using LayarTerakhir.UI;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Editor
{
    public static class PrologKosSceneBuilder
    {
        public const string SceneDirectory = "Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scenes";
        public const string ScenePath = "Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scenes/Prolog_KamarKos.unity";

        [MenuItem("LayarTerakhir/Build Prolog Kamar Kos Scene")]
        public static void BuildSceneMenu()
        {
            BuildSceneHeadless();
        }

        public static void BuildSceneHeadless()
        {
            if (!Directory.Exists(SceneDirectory))
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(SceneDirectory);
            }

            Scene scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            RenderSettings.ambientMode = UnityEngine.Rendering.AmbientMode.Flat;
            RenderSettings.ambientLight = new Color(0.12f, 0.13f, 0.16f);

            GameObject systemsRoot = new GameObject("GameSystems");
            systemsRoot.AddComponent<GameInputConfig>();
            systemsRoot.AddComponent<CampaignState>();
            HudController hud = systemsRoot.AddComponent<HudController>();
            hud.EnsureRuntimeUIBuilt();
            KamarKosPrologDirector director = systemsRoot.AddComponent<KamarKosPrologDirector>();

            GameObject roomRoot = new GameObject("KamarKos_4x3m_Greybox");

            CreateBox("Floor_4x3m", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(0f, -0.05f, 0f), new Vector3(4.0f, 0.1f, 3.0f), new Color(0.22f, 0.20f, 0.18f));
            CreateBox("Ceiling_4x3m", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(0f, 2.85f, 0f), new Vector3(4.0f, 0.1f, 3.0f), new Color(0.25f, 0.25f, 0.25f));
            CreateBox("Wall_North", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(0f, 1.4f, 1.55f), new Vector3(4.0f, 2.8f, 0.1f), new Color(0.32f, 0.31f, 0.28f));
            CreateBox("Wall_South", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(0f, 1.4f, -1.55f), new Vector3(4.0f, 2.8f, 0.1f), new Color(0.32f, 0.31f, 0.28f));
            CreateBox("Wall_East", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(2.05f, 1.4f, 0f), new Vector3(0.1f, 2.8f, 3.0f), new Color(0.30f, 0.29f, 0.27f));
            CreateBox("Wall_West", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(-2.05f, 1.4f, 0f), new Vector3(0.1f, 2.8f, 3.0f), new Color(0.30f, 0.29f, 0.27f));

            CreateBox("MejaKerjaPC", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(-0.8f, 0.72f, 1.15f), new Vector3(1.3f, 0.06f, 0.65f), new Color(0.28f, 0.18f, 0.10f));
            CreateBox("KakiMejaKiri", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(-1.4f, 0.35f, 1.15f), new Vector3(0.08f, 0.7f, 0.6f), new Color(0.20f, 0.13f, 0.08f));
            CreateBox("KakiMejaKanan", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(-0.2f, 0.35f, 1.15f), new Vector3(0.08f, 0.7f, 0.6f), new Color(0.20f, 0.13f, 0.08f));
            CreateBox("KasurKos", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(-1.35f, 0.22f, -0.45f), new Vector3(1.1f, 0.44f, 1.8f), new Color(0.35f, 0.38f, 0.44f));
            CreateBox("LemariPakaian", roomRoot.transform, new Vector3(1.55f, 1.0f, 0.85f), new Vector3(0.75f, 2.0f, 0.9f), new Color(0.26f, 0.17f, 0.11f));

            GameObject roomLightObj = new GameObject("RoomCeilingLight");
            roomLightObj.transform.SetParent(roomRoot.transform, false);
            roomLightObj.transform.position = new Vector3(0f, 2.65f, 0f);
            Light roomLight = roomLightObj.AddComponent<Light>();
            roomLight.type = LightType.Point;
            roomLight.range = 6.5f;
            roomLight.intensity = 0.85f;
            roomLight.color = new Color(1.0f, 0.88f, 0.72f);

            GameObject pcLightObj = new GameObject("PCMonitorGlow");
            pcLightObj.transform.SetParent(roomRoot.transform, false);
            pcLightObj.transform.position = new Vector3(-0.8f, 1.05f, 1.1f);
            Light pcLight = pcLightObj.AddComponent<Light>();
            pcLight.type = LightType.Point;
            pcLight.range = 2.5f;
            pcLight.intensity = 0.65f;
            pcLight.color = new Color(0.45f, 0.75f, 1.0f);

            director.pcMonitorItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_PCMonitor",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(-0.8f, 1.02f, 1.25f),
                new Vector3(0.55f, 0.36f, 0.06f),
                new Color(0.10f, 0.16f, 0.24f),
                "PC_MONITOR",
                "Periksa Monitor PC (Kanal Malam Keramat)",
                director
            );

            director.stickyNoteItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_StickyNote",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(-0.42f, 0.77f, 1.05f),
                new Vector3(0.14f, 0.02f, 0.14f),
                new Color(0.95f, 0.90f, 0.30f),
                "STICKY_NOTE",
                "Baca Sticky Note di Meja PC",
                director
            );

            director.lockedDrawerItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_LaciMejaTerkunci",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(-0.45f, 0.56f, 0.82f),
                new Vector3(0.42f, 0.22f, 0.08f),
                new Color(0.36f, 0.22f, 0.12f),
                "LACI_MEJA",
                "Buka Laci Meja Kerja (Gembok Angka 3 Digit)",
                director
            );

            director.wallCalendarItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_KalenderDinding",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(0.75f, 1.45f, 1.48f),
                new Vector3(0.38f, 0.52f, 0.03f),
                new Color(0.90f, 0.88f, 0.85f),
                "KALENDER_DINDING",
                "Periksa Kalender Dinding (Sebelah Lemari)",
                director
            );

            director.suratKosItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_SuratIbuKos",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(0.85f, 0.02f, -1.32f),
                new Vector3(0.28f, 0.02f, 0.22f),
                new Color(0.85f, 0.18f, 0.18f),
                "SURAT_KOS",
                "Ambil Kertas Merah di Bawah Pintu Kamar",
                director
            );

            director.smartphoneBedItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_SmartphoneDiKasur",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(-1.15f, 0.46f, -0.35f),
                new Vector3(0.12f, 0.03f, 0.22f),
                new Color(0.12f, 0.12f, 0.15f),
                "SMARTPHONE_BED",
                "Ambil Smartphone di Atas Kasur",
                director
            );

            GameObject redLedObj = new GameObject("SmartphoneRedLED");
            redLedObj.transform.SetParent(director.smartphoneBedItem.transform, false);
            redLedObj.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 0.12f, 0f);
            Light redLed = redLedObj.AddComponent<Light>();
            redLed.type = LightType.Point;
            redLed.color = Color.red;
            redLed.range = 1.4f;
            redLed.intensity = 2.0f;
            redLed.enabled = false;
            director.phoneBedRedLed = redLed;

            director.kosDoorItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_PintuKamarKos",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(0.85f, 1.05f, -1.48f),
                new Vector3(0.92f, 2.1f, 0.08f),
                new Color(0.33f, 0.20f, 0.12f),
                "PINTU_KOS",
                "Buka Pintu Kamar Kos (Berangkat ke Rumah Atmaja)",
                director
            );

            director.jacketItem = CreateInteractableBox(
                "Interactable_JaketDiBalikPintu",
                roomRoot.transform,
                new Vector3(1.12f, 1.35f, -1.42f),
                new Vector3(0.32f, 0.65f, 0.10f),
                new Color(0.16f, 0.24f, 0.18f),
                "JAKET_PINTU",
                "Ambil Jaket di Balik Pintu Kamar",
                director
            );

            GameObject playerObj = new GameObject("Player_RakaWijaya");
            playerObj.tag = "Player";
            playerObj.transform.position = new Vector3(-0.8f, 0.05f, 0.25f);
            playerObj.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 0f);

            CharacterController cc = playerObj.AddComponent<CharacterController>();
            cc.radius = 0.32f;
            cc.height = 1.75f;
            cc.center = new Vector3(0f, 0.875f, 0f);

            FirstPersonController fpp = playerObj.AddComponent<FirstPersonController>();

            GameObject camPivot = new GameObject("CameraPivot");
            camPivot.transform.SetParent(playerObj.transform, false);
            camPivot.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 1.6f, 0f);
            fpp.cameraPivot = camPivot.transform;

            GameObject camObj = new GameObject("MainCamera");
            camObj.tag = "MainCamera";
            camObj.transform.SetParent(camPivot.transform, false);
            Camera mainCam = camObj.AddComponent<Camera>();
            mainCam.nearClipPlane = 0.05f;
            mainCam.fieldOfView = 68f;
            camObj.AddComponent<AudioListener>();

            InteractionInteractor interactor = playerObj.AddComponent<InteractionInteractor>();
            interactor.playerCamera = mainCam;

            SmartphoneController phoneCtrl = playerObj.AddComponent<SmartphoneController>();
            GameObject handPhoneMesh = CreateBox(
                "HandSmartphoneRig",
                camObj.transform,
                new Vector3(0.24f, -0.18f, 0.38f),
                new Vector3(0.08f, 0.15f, 0.02f),
                new Color(0.10f, 0.10f, 0.12f),
                withCollider: false
            );
            phoneCtrl.handPhoneMeshRoot = handPhoneMesh;

            GameObject flashObj = new GameObject("SmartphoneFlashlight");
            flashObj.transform.SetParent(handPhoneMesh.transform, false);
            flashObj.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0.05f);
            Light flashLight = flashObj.AddComponent<Light>();
            flashLight.type = LightType.Spot;
            flashLight.spotAngle = 58f;
            flashLight.range = 10f;
            flashLight.intensity = 1.4f;
            phoneCtrl.phoneFlashlight = flashLight;

            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, ScenePath);
            RegisterSceneInBuildSettings(ScenePath);
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            Debug.Log($"[PrologKosSceneBuilder] Scene berhasil dibuat dan disimpan di: {ScenePath}");
        }

        private static void RegisterSceneInBuildSettings(string scenePath)
        {
            var existing = EditorBuildSettings.scenes;
            foreach (var s in existing)
            {
                if (s.path == scenePath)
                    return;
            }

            var updated = new EditorBuildSettingsScene[existing.Length + 1];
            for (int i = 0; i < existing.Length; i++)
                updated[i] = existing[i];

            updated[existing.Length] = new EditorBuildSettingsScene(scenePath, true);
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = updated;
        }

        private static KosInteractableItem CreateInteractableBox(
            string name,
            Transform parent,
            Vector3 position,
            Vector3 scale,
            Color color,
            string itemId,
            string prompt,
            KamarKosPrologDirector director)
        {
            GameObject go = CreateBox(name, parent, position, scale, color, withCollider: true);
            KosInteractableItem item = go.AddComponent<KosInteractableItem>();
            item.itemId = itemId;
            item.promptLabel = prompt;
            item.director = director;
            return item;
        }

        private static GameObject CreateBox(
            string name,
            Transform parent,
            Vector3 position,
            Vector3 scale,
            Color color,
            bool withCollider = true)
        {
            GameObject go = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(PrimitiveType.Cube);
            go.name = name;
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            go.transform.position = position;
            go.transform.localScale = scale;

            if (!withCollider)
            {
                Collider col = go.GetComponent<Collider>();
                if (col != null)
                    Object.DestroyImmediate(col);
            }

            Renderer rend = go.GetComponent<Renderer>();
            if (rend != null)
            {
                Shader shader = Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Lit");
                if (shader == null) shader = Shader.Find("Standard");
                if (shader != null)
                {
                    Material mat = new Material(shader);
                    mat.color = color;
                    if (mat.HasProperty("_BaseColor"))
                        mat.SetColor("_BaseColor", color);
                    rend.sharedMaterial = mat;
                }
            }

            return go;
        }
    }
}
#endif
CS_EOF

cat << 'CS_EOF' > "$PROJECT_ROOT/Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Editor/PrologMilestoneValidator.cs"
#if UNITY_EDITOR
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using LayarTerakhir.Core;
using LayarTerakhir.Player;
using LayarTerakhir.Prolog;
using LayarTerakhir.Smartphone;
using LayarTerakhir.UI;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Editor
{
    public static class PrologMilestoneValidator
    {
        public static void RunValidation()
        {
            int failures = 0;
            Debug.Log("=== [VALIDATOR] MEMULAI VALIDASI MILESTONE 1 LAYAR TERAKHIR ===");

            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(PrologKosSceneBuilder.ScenePath))
                {
                    Debug.Log("[VALIDATOR] Scene belum ada, menjalankan PrologKosSceneBuilder.BuildSceneHeadless()...");
                    PrologKosSceneBuilder.BuildSceneHeadless();
                }

                Check(File.Exists(PrologKosSceneBuilder.ScenePath), "File scene Prolog_KamarKos.unity tersedia", ref failures);

                var scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(PrologKosSceneBuilder.ScenePath, OpenSceneMode.Single);
                Check(scene.IsValid() && scene.isLoaded, "Scene Prolog_KamarKos.unity berhasil dibuka", ref failures);

                var inputCfg = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<GameInputConfig>();
                Check(inputCfg != null, "GameInputConfig ditemukan di scene", ref failures);

                var campaign = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<CampaignState>();
                Check(campaign != null, "CampaignState ditemukan di scene", ref failures);

                var hud = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<HudController>();
                Check(hud != null && hud.objectiveText != null && hud.padlockPanel != null, "HudController beserta panel runtime UI terkonfigurasi", ref failures);

                var fpp = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<FirstPersonController>();
                Check(fpp != null && fpp.cameraPivot != null, "FirstPersonController dan CameraPivot terhubung", ref failures);

                var interactor = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<InteractionInteractor>();
                Check(interactor != null && interactor.playerCamera != null, "InteractionInteractor dan MainCamera terhubung", ref failures);

                var phone = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<SmartphoneController>();
                Check(phone != null && phone.handPhoneMeshRoot != null, "SmartphoneController dan rig tangan terhubung", ref failures);

                var director = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<KamarKosPrologDirector>();
                Check(director != null, "KamarKosPrologDirector ditemukan di scene", ref failures);
                Check(KamarKosPrologDirector.DrawerPasscode == "148", "Kode gembok laci sesuai GDD (148)", ref failures);

                string[] requiredIds =
                {
                    "PC_MONITOR",
                    "SURAT_KOS",
                    "SMARTPHONE_BED",
                    "STICKY_NOTE",
                    "KALENDER_DINDING",
                    "LACI_MEJA",
                    "JAKET_PINTU",
                    "PINTU_KOS"
                };

                var allInteractables = UnityEngine.Object.FindObjectsByType<KosInteractableItem>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
                var foundIds = new HashSet<string>();
                foreach (var item in allInteractables)
                {
                    if (item != null && item.GetComponent<Collider>() != null && item.director == director)
                    {
                        foundIds.Add(item.itemId);
                    }
                }

                foreach (string reqId in requiredIds)
                {
                    Check(foundIds.Contains(reqId), $"Interactable '{reqId}' memiliki Collider dan terhubung ke Director", ref failures);
                }

                GameObject floor = GameObject.Find("Floor_4x3m");
                Check(
                    floor != null &&
                    Mathf.Approximately(floor.transform.localScale.x, 4.0f) &&
                    Mathf.Approximately(floor.transform.localScale.z, 3.0f),
                    "Dimensi lantai Kamar Kos sesuai spesifikasi GDD (4x3 meter)",
                    ref failures
                );

                if (campaign != null)
                {
                    campaign.StartNewGame();
                    campaign.AddInventoryItem("KUNCI_MOTOR");
                    bool saved = campaign.SaveCheckpointAtomic("VALIDATOR_TEST_CHECKPOINT");
                    bool loaded = campaign.LoadCheckpoint();
                    Check(
                        saved && loaded &&
                        campaign.Data.checkpoint_id == "VALIDATOR_TEST_CHECKPOINT" &&
                        campaign.HasInventoryItem("KUNCI_MOTOR"),
                        "Penyimpanan dan pemulihan checkpoint JSON atomik berfungsi",
                        ref failures
                    );
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                failures++;
                Debug.LogError($"[VALIDATOR] [FAIL] Exception saat validasi: {ex}");
            }

            if (failures == 0)
            {
                Debug.Log("=== [VALIDATOR] SEMUA PEMERIKSAAN LULUS (0 GAGAL) ===");
                EditorApplication.Exit(0);
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.LogError($"=== [VALIDATOR] VALIDASI GAGAL DENGAN {failures} KESALAHAN ===");
                EditorApplication.Exit(1);
            }
        }

        private static void Check(bool condition, string description, ref int failures)
        {
            if (condition)
            {
                Debug.Log($"[VALIDATOR] [PASS] {description}");
            }
            else
            {
                failures++;
                Debug.LogError($"[VALIDATOR] [FAIL] {description}");
            }
        }
    }
}
#endif

## 4. Perintah Kompilasi, Pembuatan Scene, dan Validasi Headless

Setelah menjalankan `install_milestone1.sh`, jalankan perintah berikut di VPS Ubuntu 24.04 Anda (sesuaikan variabel `UNITY_BIN` jika binary `6000.3.26f1` Anda diinstal di lokasi berbeda):

```bash
UNITY_BIN="/opt/unity/Editor/Unity"
# Jika diinstal lewat Unity Hub default di root:
# UNITY_BIN="/root/Unity/Hub/Editor/6000.3.26f1/Editor/Unity"

PROJECT_PATH="/root/Projects/LayarTerakhir"
LOG_FILE="/root/Projects/LayarTerakhir/milestone1_validation.log"

"$UNITY_BIN" \
  -batchmode \
  -nographics \
  -quit \
  -projectPath "$PROJECT_PATH" \
  -executeMethod LayarTerakhir.Editor.PrologMilestoneValidator.RunValidation \
  -logFile "$LOG_FILE"

echo "Exit Code: $?"
grep -E "\[VALIDATOR\]|error CS|Exception" "$LOG_FILE"

```

### Cara Melaporkan Hasil Validasi Kembali:

Karena saya tidak memiliki akses langsung ke VPS Anda, jalankan perintah `grep` di atas dan tempelkan keluaran dari `milestone1_validation.log` beserta `Exit Code` ke obrolan ini. Jika terdapat pesan `error CS...` atau `[VALIDATOR] [FAIL]`, kita akan memperbaikinya sebelum melangkah ke Milestone 2.

## 5. Instruksi Pengujian Hasil Build (Windows PC)

Untuk membangun *executable* Windows PC (`LayarTerakhir_PrologKos.exe`) secara *headless* dari VPS atau membukanya di PC Windows Anda:

1. **Membuka Scene di Unity Editor (atau Membangun Player):**
* Scene `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scenes/Prolog_KamarKos.unity` sudah terdaftar secara otomatis di `EditorBuildSettings.scenes` oleh `PrologKosSceneBuilder`.


2. **Urutan Pengujian Gameplay Prolog Adegan 1 (Menit 00:00 – 12:00):**
* **Pembukaan:** Layar memudar dari hitam di depan monitor PC, menampilkan monolog Raka tentang 32 penonton diikuti teks suara ketukan keras 3 kali di pintu dan objektif `Periksa bawah pintu kamar.`

* **Interaksi Surat Ibu Kos:** Berjalan (`WASD`) ke pintu selatan, arahkan ke kertas merah di lantai bawah pintu, tekan `E` untuk membaca **Surat Peringatan Terakhir Bu Haji Ratna** (`Rp3.500.000`), lalu tutup dokumen.


* **Mengambil Smartphone:** Lampu LED merah di atas kasur berkedip; arahkan ke *Smartphone* di kasur dan tekan `E` untuk memutar **Voice Note Damar**, diikuti *glitch* dan notifikasi donasi `Rp5.000.000` dari `Penonton_Terakhir`.


* **Uji Mekanik Smartphone:** Tekan `TAB` untuk menyimpan/mengangkat HP, dan tekan `F` untuk mematikan/menyalakan layar HP (perhatikan pengurasan baterai berhenti saat layar mati).


* **Puzzle Laci Terkunci (`148`):** Periksa *Sticky Note* di meja PC dan *Kalender Dinding* di sebelah lemari (14 Agustus — Ultah Ibu), lalu tekan `E` pada laci meja kerja dan masukkan kombinasi `1 - 4 - 8` untuk memperoleh **Kunci Motor** dan **1x Baterai Cadangan (Powerbank Kecil)**.


* **Persiapan Akhir & Transisi:** Ambil **Jaket** di balik pintu kamar (`E`), lalu berinteraksi dengan **Pintu Kamar Kos** (`E`) untuk memicu penyimpanan checkpoint atomik (`PROLOG_KOS_COMPLETE`) dan *Fade to Black*.

## 6. Daftar Fitur yang Belum Diimplementasikan / Belum Diuji (Di Luar Milestone 1)

Sesuai batasan lingkup bertahap agar implementasi tetap teruji dan terkelola:

* **Prolog Adegan 2 – 7 (Rumah Keluarga Atmaja):** Area halaman teras, dapur/sumur, ruang makan (pengambilan chip `MataBatin.apk` & pembukaan fitur Lensa AR), puzzle jam bandul `03:45`, teka-teki Tiga Dosa di lantai 2, *stealth* ruang jahit bawah tanah (gangguan baterai 12%, *locker check* Nyonya Sri Atmaja), serta *Grand Chase* dan *Blitz* darurat 4 detik belum dibuat.


* **Aset Audio & 3D Final:** Saat ini masih menggunakan geometri *greybox* prosedural dan *cue* teks *subtitle* sebagai *placeholder* untuk suara hujan, derit pintu, *voice note*, dan model 3D.


* **Sistem AI Entitas (`GhostBase`) & Simulasi Live Chat Dinamis:** Baru akan diaktifkan pada Milestone berikutnya saat Raka tiba di Rumah Keluarga Atmaja dan menyalakan `[MULAI LIVE]`.




Implementasi **Milestone 1 (Fondasi Teknis & Prolog Adegan 1: Kamar Kos Raka)** dirancang menggunakan arsitektur *code-first* untuk **Unity 6.3 LTS (6000.3.26f1)** pada lingkungan *headless batch mode* Ubuntu 24.04 tanpa mengubah `ProjectSettings`, `Packages`, maupun konfigurasi URP yang sudah ada.

Seluruh mekanik, kontrol, struktur data simpanan, ukuran ruangan kos (4×3 meter), dialog monolog Raka, pesan suara Damar, intervensi donasi `Penonton_Terakhir`, serta teka-teki gembok laci (`148`) mengikuti dokumen rancangan asli secara presisi.

## 2. Kode Sumber Lengkap (C#)

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Core/GameInputConfig.cs`

Menangani kontrol baku sesuai GDD (`WASD`, `SHIFT` sprint, `CTRL` crouch/slide, `SPACE` vault, `E` interaksi, `TAB` angkat/simpan HP, `F` layar mati/nyala, `Q` AR, `R` UV, `V` Selfie, `T` ThermoKlenik, `C` EVP, `J` Jurnal, `B` powerbank, `G` flare, `H` kemenyan, `LMB` foto/Blitz) dan mendukung pemetaan ulang tombol. Menggunakan kompilasi kondisional agar kompatibel dengan *Input System Package* baru di Unity 6.3 maupun *Legacy Input Manager*.

```csharp
using System;
using UnityEngine;
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
#endif

namespace LayarTerakhir.Core
{
    public enum GameAction
    {
        MoveForward,
        MoveBackward,
        MoveLeft,
        MoveRight,
        Sprint,
        Crouch,
        Vault,
        Interact,
        TogglePhone,
        ToggleScreen,
        ModeAR,
        ModeUV,
        ModeSelfie,
        ModeThermo,
        ModeEVP,
        OpenJournal,
        UsePowerbank,
        UseFlare,
        UseIncense,
        CaptureOrBlitz
    }

    [Serializable]
    public class GameInputConfig : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static GameInputConfig Instance { get; private set; }

        [Header("Remappable Keybindings (GDD Standard)")]
        public KeyCode moveForwardKey = KeyCode.W;
        public KeyCode moveBackwardKey = KeyCode.S;
        public KeyCode moveLeftKey = KeyCode.A;
        public KeyCode moveRightKey = KeyCode.D;
        public KeyCode sprintKey = KeyCode.LeftShift;
        public KeyCode crouchKey = KeyCode.LeftControl;
        public KeyCode vaultKey = KeyCode.Space;
        public KeyCode interactKey = KeyCode.E;
        public KeyCode togglePhoneKey = KeyCode.Tab;
        public KeyCode toggleScreenKey = KeyCode.F;
        public KeyCode modeARKey = KeyCode.Q;
        public KeyCode modeUVKey = KeyCode.R;
        public KeyCode modeSelfieKey = KeyCode.V;
        public KeyCode modeThermoKey = KeyCode.T;
        public KeyCode modeEVPKey = KeyCode.C;
        public KeyCode openJournalKey = KeyCode.J;
        public KeyCode usePowerbankKey = KeyCode.B;
        public KeyCode useFlareKey = KeyCode.G;
        public KeyCode useIncenseKey = KeyCode.H;
        public KeyCode captureOrBlitzKey = KeyCode.Mouse0;

        [Header("Mouse Sensitivity")]
        public float mouseSensitivity = 2.0f;

        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }
            Instance = this;
        }

        public void RemapAction(GameAction action, KeyCode newKey)
        {
            switch (action)
            {
                case GameAction.MoveForward: moveForwardKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.MoveBackward: moveBackwardKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.MoveLeft: moveLeftKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.MoveRight: moveRightKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.Sprint: sprintKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.Crouch: crouchKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.Vault: vaultKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.Interact: interactKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.TogglePhone: togglePhoneKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.ToggleScreen: toggleScreenKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.ModeAR: modeARKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.ModeUV: modeUVKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.ModeSelfie: modeSelfieKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.ModeThermo: modeThermoKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.ModeEVP: modeEVPKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.OpenJournal: openJournalKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.UsePowerbank: usePowerbankKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.UseFlare: useFlareKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.UseIncense: useIncenseKey = newKey; break;
                case GameAction.CaptureOrBlitz: captureOrBlitzKey = newKey; break;
            }
        }

        public KeyCode GetKeyForAction(GameAction action)
        {
            return action switch
            {
                GameAction.MoveForward => moveForwardKey,
                GameAction.MoveBackward => moveBackwardKey,
                GameAction.MoveLeft => moveLeftKey,
                GameAction.MoveRight => moveRightKey,
                GameAction.Sprint => sprintKey,
                GameAction.Crouch => crouchKey,
                GameAction.Vault => vaultKey,
                GameAction.Interact => interactKey,
                GameAction.TogglePhone => togglePhoneKey,
                GameAction.ToggleScreen => toggleScreenKey,
                GameAction.ModeAR => modeARKey,
                GameAction.ModeUV => modeUVKey,
                GameAction.ModeSelfie => modeSelfieKey,
                GameAction.ModeThermo => modeThermoKey,
                GameAction.ModeEVP => modeEVPKey,
                GameAction.OpenJournal => openJournalKey,
                GameAction.UsePowerbank => usePowerbankKey,
                GameAction.UseFlare => useFlareKey,
                GameAction.UseIncense => useIncenseKey,
                GameAction.CaptureOrBlitz => captureOrBlitzKey,
                _ => KeyCode.None
            };
        }

        public bool GetActionDown(GameAction action) => ReadKeyDown(GetKeyForAction(action));
        public bool GetActionHeld(GameAction action) => ReadKeyHeld(GetKeyForAction(action));
        public bool GetActionUp(GameAction action) => ReadKeyUp(GetKeyForAction(action));

        public Vector2 GetMovementInput()
        {
            float x = 0f;
            float y = 0f;
            if (GetActionHeld(GameAction.MoveRight)) x += 1f;
            if (GetActionHeld(GameAction.MoveLeft)) x -= 1f;
            if (GetActionHeld(GameAction.MoveForward)) y += 1f;
            if (GetActionHeld(GameAction.MoveBackward)) y -= 1f;

            Vector2 move = new Vector2(x, y);
            return move.sqrMagnitude > 1f ? move.normalized : move;
        }

        public Vector2 GetLookDelta()
        {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            if (Mouse.current != null)
            {
                Vector2 delta = Mouse.current.delta.ReadValue();
                return delta * (mouseSensitivity * 0.1f);
            }
#endif
#if ENABLE_LEGACY_INPUT_MANAGER
            return new Vector2(
                Input.GetAxisRaw("Mouse X") * mouseSensitivity,
                Input.GetAxisRaw("Mouse Y") * mouseSensitivity
            );
#else
            return Vector2.zero;
#endif
        }

        private static bool ReadKeyDown(KeyCode keyCode)
        {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            if (TryReadInputSystemKey(keyCode, out bool down, out _, out _))
                return down;
#endif
#if ENABLE_LEGACY_INPUT_MANAGER
            return Input.GetKeyDown(keyCode);
#else
            return false;
#endif
        }

        private static bool ReadKeyHeld(KeyCode keyCode)
        {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            if (TryReadInputSystemKey(keyCode, out _, out bool held, out _))
                return held;
#endif
#if ENABLE_LEGACY_INPUT_MANAGER
            return Input.GetKey(keyCode);
#else
            return false;
#endif
        }

        private static bool ReadKeyUp(KeyCode keyCode)
        {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            if (TryReadInputSystemKey(keyCode, out _, out _, out bool up))
                return up;
#endif
#if ENABLE_LEGACY_INPUT_MANAGER
            return Input.GetKeyUp(keyCode);
#else
            return false;
#endif
        }

#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
        private static bool TryReadInputSystemKey(KeyCode keyCode, out bool down, out bool held, out bool up)
        {
            down = false;
            held = false;
            up = false;

            if (keyCode == KeyCode.Mouse0)
            {
                if (Mouse.current == null) return false;
                down = Mouse.current.leftButton.wasPressedThisFrame;
                held = Mouse.current.leftButton.isPressed;
                up = Mouse.current.leftButton.wasReleasedThisFrame;
                return true;
            }

            if (Keyboard.current == null) return false;

            Key mapped = keyCode switch
            {
                KeyCode.W => Key.W,
                KeyCode.A => Key.A,
                KeyCode.S => Key.S,
                KeyCode.D => Key.D,
                KeyCode.E => Key.E,
                KeyCode.F => Key.F,
                KeyCode.Q => Key.Q,
                KeyCode.R => Key.R,
                KeyCode.V => Key.V,
                KeyCode.T => Key.T,
                KeyCode.C => Key.C,
                KeyCode.J => Key.J,
                KeyCode.B => Key.B,
                KeyCode.G => Key.G,
                KeyCode.H => Key.H,
                KeyCode.Tab => Key.Tab,
                KeyCode.Space => Key.Space,
                KeyCode.LeftShift => Key.LeftShift,
                KeyCode.LeftControl => Key.LeftCtrl,
                KeyCode.Escape => Key.Escape,
                _ => Key.None
            };

            if (mapped == Key.None) return false;
            var control = Keyboard.current[mapped];
            down = control.wasPressedThisFrame;
            held = control.isPressed;
            up = control.wasReleasedThisFrame;
            return true;
        }
#endif
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Core/CampaignState.cs`

Mengimplementasikan kontrak data campaign (`chapter_id`, `checkpoint_id`, `evidence_ids`, `relic_ids`, `location_seals`, `upgrades`, `inventory`, `battery`, `sanity`, `wallet_legitimate`, `wallet_bound`, `spent`, `secret_items`, `secret_notes_read`, `archive_prepared`, `ending`, dan `schema_version`) beserta penyimpanan file sementara atomik sesuai bagian *Kontrak data dan pemulihan* pada GDD.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Core
{
    public enum EndingType
    {
        None = 0,
        Bad = 1,
        Normal = 2,
        True = 3,
        Secret = 4
    }

    [Serializable]
    public class CampaignData
    {
        public int chapter_id = 0;
        public string checkpoint_id = "PROLOG_KOS_START";
        public List<string> evidence_ids = new List<string>();
        public List<string> relic_ids = new List<string>();
        public List<string> location_seals = new List<string>();
        public List<string> upgrades = new List<string>();
        public List<string> inventory = new List<string>();
        public float battery = 100f;
        public float sanity = 100f;
        public long wallet_legitimate = 0;
        public long wallet_bound = 0;
        public long spent = 0;
        public List<string> secret_items = new List<string>();
        public List<string> secret_notes_read = new List<string>();
        public bool archive_prepared = false;
        public EndingType ending = EndingType.None;
        public int schema_version = 1;
    }

    public class CampaignState : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static CampaignState Instance { get; private set; }

        public CampaignData Data { get; private set; } = new CampaignData();

        private string SaveFilePath => Path.Combine(Application.persistentDataPath, "layar_terakhir_save.json");
        private string TempSaveFilePath => Path.Combine(Application.persistentDataPath, "layar_terakhir_save.json.tmp");

        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }
            Instance = this;
        }

        public void StartNewGame()
        {
            Data = new CampaignData();
        }

        public bool HasInventoryItem(string itemId)
        {
            return Data.inventory.Contains(itemId);
        }

        public bool AddInventoryItem(string itemId)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(itemId) || Data.inventory.Contains(itemId))
                return false;

            Data.inventory.Add(itemId);
            return true;
        }

        public void AddBoundDonation(long amount)
        {
            if (amount > 0)
            {
                Data.wallet_bound += amount;
            }
        }

        public bool SaveCheckpointAtomic(string checkpointId)
        {
            try
            {
                Data.checkpoint_id = checkpointId;
                string json = JsonUtility.ToJson(Data, true);
                File.WriteAllText(TempSaveFilePath, json);

                if (File.Exists(SaveFilePath))
                {
                    File.Delete(SaveFilePath);
                }
                File.Move(TempSaveFilePath, SaveFilePath);
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.LogError($"[CampaignState] Gagal menyimpan checkpoint secara atomik: {ex.Message}");
                return false;
            }
        }

        public bool LoadCheckpoint()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(SaveFilePath))
                    return false;

                string json = File.ReadAllText(SaveFilePath);
                CampaignData loaded = JsonUtility.FromJson<CampaignData>(json);
                if (loaded == null || loaded.schema_version != 1)
                    return false;

                Data = loaded;
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Debug.LogError($"[CampaignState] File simpan rusak atau gagal dimuat: {ex.Message}");
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Player/InteractionInteractor.cs`

Melakukan *raycast* dari kamera *First-Person* untuk mendeteksi objek `IInteractable`, menampilkan *prompt* kontekstual di layar, dan mengeksekusi interaksi saat pemain menekan `E`.

```csharp
using LayarTerakhir.Core;
using LayarTerakhir.UI;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Player
{
    public class InteractionInteractor : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Header("Raycast Configuration")]
        public Camera playerCamera;
        public float interactionDistance = 2.6f;
        public LayerMask interactableMask = ~0;

        public IInteractable CurrentTarget { get; private set; }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (HudController.Instance != null && HudController.Instance.IsModalOpen)
            {
                CurrentTarget = null;
                HudController.Instance.SetInteractionPrompt(string.Empty);
                return;
            }

            UpdateRaycastTarget();

            if (CurrentTarget != null && GameInputConfig.Instance != null &&
                GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.Interact))
            {
                CurrentTarget.Interact();
            }
        }

        private void UpdateRaycastTarget()
        {
            CurrentTarget = null;
            if (playerCamera == null)
                return;

            Ray ray = new Ray(playerCamera.transform.position, playerCamera.transform.forward);
            if (Physics.Raycast(ray, out RaycastHit hit, interactionDistance, interactableMask, QueryTriggerInteraction.Collide))
            {
                IInteractable interactable = hit.collider.GetComponentInParent<IInteractable>();
                if (interactable != null && interactable.CanInteract())
                {
                    CurrentTarget = interactable;
                    if (HudController.Instance != null)
                    {
                        HudController.Instance.SetInteractionPrompt($"[E] {interactable.GetInteractionPrompt()}");
                    }
                    return;
                }
            }

            if (HudController.Instance != null)
            {
                HudController.Instance.SetInteractionPrompt(string.Empty);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Player/FirstPersonController.cs`

Mengimplementasikan kontroler FPP (`CORE-01`) dengan `WASD`, *mouse look*, *sprint* (`SHIFT`), *crouch* (`CTRL`), *slide* saat *sprint*, *vault* (`SPACE`), deteksi tabrakan `CharacterController`, serta gravitasi.

```csharp
using LayarTerakhir.Core;
using LayarTerakhir.UI;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Player
{
    [RequireComponent(typeof(CharacterController))]
    public class FirstPersonController : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [Header("References")]
        public Transform cameraPivot;

        [Header("Movement Speeds")]
        public float walkSpeed = 2.8f;
        public float sprintSpeed = 4.6f;
        public float crouchSpeed = 1.6f;
        public float slideSpeed = 5.5f;
        public float slideDuration = 0.65f;

        [Header("Posture & Gravity")]
        public float standingHeight = 1.75f;
        public float crouchHeight = 1.0f;
        public float cameraStandingY = 1.6f;
        public float cameraCrouchY = 0.85f;
        public float postureLerpSpeed = 10f;
        public float gravity = -19.62f;
        public float vaultImpulse = 4.8f;

        [Header("Look Limits")]
        public float minPitch = -80f;
        public float maxPitch = 80f;

        public bool MovementLocked { get; set; }
        public bool IsCrouching { get; private set; }
        public bool IsSprinting { get; private set; }
        public bool IsSliding { get; private set; }

        private CharacterController _controller;
        private float _pitch;
        private Vector3 _verticalVelocity;
        private float _slideTimer;
        private Vector3 _slideDirection;

        private void Awake()
        {
            _controller = GetComponent<CharacterController>();
            _controller.height = standingHeight;
            _controller.center = new Vector3(0f, standingHeight * 0.5f, 0f);
        }

        private void Start()
        {
            SetCursorLock(true);
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            bool modalOpen = HudController.Instance != null && HudController.Instance.IsModalOpen;
            if (modalOpen || MovementLocked)
            {
                ApplyGravityOnly();
                return;
            }

            HandleMouseLook();
            HandleMovementAndPosture();
        }

        public void SetCursorLock(bool locked)
        {
            Cursor.lockState = locked ? CursorLockMode.Locked : CursorLockMode.None;
            Cursor.visible = !locked;
        }

        private void HandleMouseLook()
        {
            if (GameInputConfig.Instance == null || cameraPivot == null)
                return;

            Vector2 look = GameInputConfig.Instance.GetLookDelta();
            transform.Rotate(Vector3.up * look.x);

            _pitch = Mathf.Clamp(_pitch - look.y, minPitch, maxPitch);
            cameraPivot.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(_pitch, 0f, 0f);
        }

        private void HandleMovementAndPosture()
        {
            if (GameInputConfig.Instance == null)
                return;

            Vector2 input = GameInputConfig.Instance.GetMovementInput();
            Vector3 desiredMove = (transform.right * input.x + transform.forward * input.y);
            if (desiredMove.sqrMagnitude > 1f)
                desiredMove.Normalize();

            bool crouchHeld = GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionHeld(GameAction.Crouch);
            bool crouchDown = GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.Crouch);
            bool sprintHeld = GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionHeld(GameAction.Sprint);

            if (IsSliding)
            {
                _slideTimer -= Time.deltaTime;
                if (_slideTimer <= 0f)
                {
                    IsSliding = false;
                }
            }
            else if (crouchDown && sprintHeld && input.y > 0.5f && _controller.isGrounded)
            {
                IsSliding = true;
                _slideTimer = slideDuration;
                _slideDirection = desiredMove.sqrMagnitude > 0.01f ? desiredMove : transform.forward;
            }

            IsCrouching = crouchHeld || IsSliding || !CanStandUp();
            IsSprinting = !IsCrouching && sprintHeld && input.y > 0.1f;

            float targetHeight = IsCrouching ? crouchHeight : standingHeight;
            _controller.height = Mathf.Lerp(_controller.height, targetHeight, Time.deltaTime * postureLerpSpeed);
            _controller.center = new Vector3(0f, _controller.height * 0.5f, 0f);

            if (cameraPivot != null)
            {
                float targetCamY = IsCrouching ? cameraCrouchY : cameraStandingY;
                Vector3 localPos = cameraPivot.localPosition;
                localPos.y = Mathf.Lerp(localPos.y, targetCamY, Time.deltaTime * postureLerpSpeed);
                cameraPivot.localPosition = localPos;
            }

            float currentSpeed = walkSpeed;
            if (IsSliding) currentSpeed = slideSpeed;
            else if (IsCrouching) currentSpeed = crouchSpeed;
            else if (IsSprinting) currentSpeed = sprintSpeed;

            Vector3 horizontalVelocity = IsSliding ? _slideDirection * currentSpeed : desiredMove * currentSpeed;

            if (_controller.isGrounded)
            {
                if (_verticalVelocity.y < 0f)
                    _verticalVelocity.y = -2f;

                if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.Vault) && !IsCrouching)
                {
                    _verticalVelocity.y = vaultImpulse;
                }
            }

            _verticalVelocity.y += gravity * Time.deltaTime;
            Vector3 finalMotion = horizontalVelocity + _verticalVelocity;
            _controller.Move(finalMotion * Time.deltaTime);
        }

        private void ApplyGravityOnly()
        {
            if (_controller.isGrounded && _verticalVelocity.y < 0f)
                _verticalVelocity.y = -2f;

            _verticalVelocity.y += gravity * Time.deltaTime;
            _controller.Move(_verticalVelocity * Time.deltaTime);
        }

        private bool CanStandUp()
        {
            if (!IsCrouching)
                return true;

            Vector3 origin = transform.position + Vector3.up * crouchHeight;
            float checkDistance = (standingHeight - crouchHeight) + 0.05f;
            return !Physics.SphereCast(origin, _controller.radius * 0.85f, Vector3.up, out _, checkDistance);
        }
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Smartphone/SmartphoneController.cs`

Mengimplementasikan pondasi Smartphone (`CORE-02` dan `CORE-03`): satu perangkat aktif, layar hidup/mati (`F`) yang terpisah dari status koneksi siaran, kamera depan/belakang (`V`), slot eksklusif AR/UV (`Q`/`R`), serta angka pengurasan baterai sesuai prototipe GDD (kamera biasa 1% per 20 detik, AR/UV 1% per 10 detik, foto 1%, Blitz 15% dengan cooldown 6 detik, layar mati menghentikan drain, baterai 0 mengaktifkan mode layar mati tanpa Game Over otomatis).

```csharp
using LayarTerakhir.Core;
using LayarTerakhir.UI;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Smartphone
{
    public enum ScanVisualMode
    {
        Normal = 0,
        AR = 1,
        UV = 2
    }

    public class SmartphoneController : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static SmartphoneController Instance { get; private set; }

        [Header("Device State")]
        public bool hasSmartphoneInInventory = false;
        public bool isPhoneRaised = false;
        public bool isScreenOn = true;
        public bool isBroadcastConnected = false;
        public bool isSelfieCamera = false;
        public ScanVisualMode currentScanMode = ScanVisualMode.Normal;

        [Header("Module Unlocks (Gated by Story Progression)")]
        public bool arModuleUnlocked = false;
        public bool uvModuleUnlocked = false;
        public bool selfieModuleUnlocked = false;

        [Header("Battery Parameters (GDD Rancangan.MD)")]
        public float batteryPercent = 100f;
        public float normalDrainPerSecond = 1f / 20f; // 1% per 20 detik
        public float arOrUvDrainPerSecond = 1f / 10f; // 1% per 10 detik
        public float photoCostPercent = 1f;           // Foto bukti 1%
        public float blitzCostPercent = 15f;          // Blitz 15%
        public float blitzCooldownSeconds = 6f;       // Cooldown 6 detik
        public float largePowerbankRestore = 80f;     // Powerbank besar +80
        public float smallPowerbankRestore = 35f;     // Powerbank kecil prolog kos

        [Header("Visual Rig References")]
        public GameObject handPhoneMeshRoot;
        public Light phoneFlashlight;

        private float _blitzCooldownTimer;

        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }
            Instance = this;
        }

        private void Start()
        {
            SyncVisualState();
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (_blitzCooldownTimer > 0f)
                _blitzCooldownTimer -= Time.deltaTime;

            if (!hasSmartphoneInInventory)
                return;

            if (HudController.Instance != null && HudController.Instance.IsModalOpen)
                return;

            HandlePhoneInput();
            UpdateBatteryDrain();
        }

        public void AcquireSmartphone()
        {
            hasSmartphoneInInventory = true;
            isPhoneRaised = true;
            isScreenOn = true;
            SyncVisualState();
        }

        private void HandlePhoneInput()
        {
            if (GameInputConfig.Instance == null)
                return;

            if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.TogglePhone))
            {
                isPhoneRaised = !isPhoneRaised;
                SyncVisualState();
            }

            if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.ToggleScreen))
            {
                if (batteryPercent > 0f)
                {
                    isScreenOn = !isScreenOn;
                    SyncVisualState();
                }
            }

            if (!isPhoneRaised || !isScreenOn)
                return;

            // AR dan UV memakai satu slot pemindaian visual dan saling menggantikan
            if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.ModeAR) && arModuleUnlocked)
            {
                currentScanMode = currentScanMode == ScanVisualMode.AR
                    ? ScanVisualMode.Normal
                    : ScanVisualMode.AR;
                SyncVisualState();
            }
            else if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.ModeUV) && uvModuleUnlocked)
            {
                currentScanMode = currentScanMode == ScanVisualMode.UV
                    ? ScanVisualMode.Normal
                    : ScanVisualMode.UV;
                SyncVisualState();
            }

            if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.ModeSelfie) && selfieModuleUnlocked)
            {
                isSelfieCamera = !isSelfieCamera;
                SyncVisualState();
            }

            if (GameInputConfig.Instance.GetActionDown(GameAction.UsePowerbank))
            {
                TryUsePowerbank();
            }
        }

        private void UpdateBatteryDrain()
        {
            // Layar mati atau menyimpan HP menghentikan drain baterai sesuai GDD
            if (!isPhoneRaised || !isScreenOn || batteryPercent <= 0f)
                return;

            float drainRate = (currentScanMode == ScanVisualMode.AR || currentScanMode == ScanVisualMode.UV)
                ? arOrUvDrainPerSecond
                : normalDrainPerSecond;

            ConsumeBattery(drainRate * Time.deltaTime);
        }

        public void ConsumeBattery(float amount)
        {
            batteryPercent = Mathf.Clamp(batteryPercent - amount, 0f, 100f);
            if (CampaignState.Instance != null)
            {
                CampaignState.Instance.Data.battery = batteryPercent;
            }

            // Battery 0 mengaktifkan mode layar mati, bukan Game Over otomatis
            if (batteryPercent <= 0f && isScreenOn)
            {
                isScreenOn = false;
                currentScanMode = ScanVisualMode.Normal;
                SyncVisualState();
            }

            if (HudController.Instance != null)
            {
                HudController.Instance.UpdatePhoneStatusUI(this);
            }
        }

        public bool TryUsePowerbank()
        {
            if (CampaignState.Instance == null || batteryPercent >= 100f)
                return false;

            if (CampaignState.Instance.Data.inventory.Remove("POWERBANK_BESAR"))
            {
                batteryPercent = Mathf.Clamp(batteryPercent + largePowerbankRestore, 0f, 100f);
                CampaignState.Instance.Data.battery = batteryPercent;
                SyncVisualState();
                return true;
            }

            if (CampaignState.Instance.Data.inventory.Remove("POWERBANK_KECIL_KOS"))
            {
                batteryPercent = Mathf.Clamp(batteryPercent + smallPowerbankRestore, 0f, 100f);
                CampaignState.Instance.Data.battery = batteryPercent;
                SyncVisualState();
                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }

        public void SyncVisualState()
        {
            if (handPhoneMeshRoot != null)
            {
                handPhoneMeshRoot.SetActive(hasSmartphoneInInventory && isPhoneRaised);
            }

            if (phoneFlashlight != null)
            {
                phoneFlashlight.enabled = hasSmartphoneInInventory && isPhoneRaised && isScreenOn && batteryPercent > 0f;
            }

            if (HudController.Instance != null)
            {
                HudController.Instance.UpdatePhoneStatusUI(this);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/UI/HudController.cs`

Membangun dan mengelola antarmuka pengguna secara terprogram (*code-first*), meliputi objektif layar, *subtitle* dialog/monolog dengan sistem antrean berwaktu, pembaca dokumen cerita, antarmuka gembok kombinasi 3 digit (`1 - 4 - 8`), tampilan notifikasi *Smartphone* (14 panggilan tak terjawab Damar, pesan suara, dan donasi `Rp5.000.000` dari `Penonton_Terakhir`), serta layar transisi *Fade to Black*.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using LayarTerakhir.Player;
using LayarTerakhir.Smartphone;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace LayarTerakhir.UI
{
    public class HudController : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static HudController Instance { get; private set; }

        [Header("UI Text Elements")]
        public Text objectiveText;
        public Text subtitleText;
        public Text interactionPromptText;
        public Text phoneStatusText;
        public Text broadcastToastText;

        [Header("Modal Panels")]
        public GameObject documentPanel;
        public Text documentTitleText;
        public Text documentBodyText;
        public Button documentCloseButton;

        public GameObject padlockPanel;
        public Text padlockDigitsText;
        public Text padlockFeedbackText;

        public Image blackFadeImage;

        public bool IsModalOpen =>
            (documentPanel != null && documentPanel.activeSelf) ||
            (padlockPanel != null && padlockPanel.activeSelf);

        private readonly Queue<(string speaker, string line, float duration)> _subtitleQueue =
            new Queue<(string, string, float)>();
        private Coroutine _subtitleRoutine;
        private Action _onDocumentClosed;
        private Action _onPadlockSolved;
        private readonly int[] _digits = { 0, 0, 0 };
        private string _targetPadlockCode = "148";

        private void Awake()
        {
            if (Instance != null && Instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }
            Instance = this;
            EnsureRuntimeUIBuilt();
        }

        public void EnsureRuntimeUIBuilt()
        {
            if (objectiveText != null && documentPanel != null && padlockPanel != null && blackFadeImage != null)
                return;

            Canvas canvas = GetComponentInChildren<Canvas>();
            if (canvas == null)
            {
                GameObject canvasObj = new GameObject("RuntimeHUDCanvas");
                canvasObj.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                canvas = canvasObj.AddComponent<Canvas>();
                canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
                CanvasScaler scaler = canvasObj.AddComponent<CanvasScaler>();
                scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
                scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1920, 1080);
                canvasObj.AddComponent<GraphicRaycaster>();
            }

            if (FindFirstObjectByType<EventSystem>() == null)
            {
                GameObject es = new GameObject("EventSystem");
                es.AddComponent<EventSystem>();
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
                es.AddComponent<UnityEngine.InputSystem.UI.InputSystemUIInputModule>();
#else
                es.AddComponent<StandaloneInputModule>();
#endif
            }

            Font defaultFont = LoadSafeFont();

            // Black Fade Overlay
            GameObject fadeObj = CreateUIObject("BlackFadeOverlay", canvas.transform);
            RectTransform fadeRect = fadeObj.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            StretchFull(fadeRect);
            blackFadeImage = fadeObj.AddComponent<Image>();
            blackFadeImage.color = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0f);
            blackFadeImage.raycastTarget = false;

            // Crosshair
            GameObject crosshairObj = CreateUIObject("Crosshair", canvas.transform);
            RectTransform crossRect = crosshairObj.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            crossRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(8f, 8f);
            Image crossImg = crosshairObj.AddComponent<Image>();
            crossImg.color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.75f);
            crossImg.raycastTarget = false;

            // Objective Banner (Top Left)
            objectiveText = CreateText("ObjectiveText", canvas.transform, defaultFont, 22, TextAnchor.UpperLeft);
            RectTransform objRect = objectiveText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            objRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
            objRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
            objRect.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
            objRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(36f, -32f);
            objRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(850f, 80f);
            objectiveText.color = new Color(1f, 0.92f, 0.65f);

            // Phone Status (Top Right)
            phoneStatusText = CreateText("PhoneStatusText", canvas.transform, defaultFont, 18, TextAnchor.UpperRight);
            RectTransform phoneRect = phoneStatusText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            phoneRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
            phoneRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
            phoneRect.pivot = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
            phoneRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-36f, -32f);
            phoneRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(560f, 110f);
            phoneStatusText.text = "[HP BELUM DIAMBIL]";

            // Broadcast / Saweran Notification Toast (Upper Center)
            broadcastToastText = CreateText("BroadcastToastText", canvas.transform, defaultFont, 20, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            RectTransform toastRect = broadcastToastText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            toastRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0.5f, 1f);
            toastRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 1f);
            toastRect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 1f);
            toastRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, -110f);
            toastRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(1100f, 110f);
            broadcastToastText.color = new Color(1f, 0.25f, 0.25f);
            broadcastToastText.text = string.Empty;

            // Interaction Prompt (Center Bottom-ish)
            interactionPromptText = CreateText("InteractionPromptText", canvas.transform, defaultFont, 22, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            RectTransform promptRect = interactionPromptText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            promptRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            promptRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            promptRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, -65f);
            promptRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(700f, 50f);
            interactionPromptText.text = string.Empty;

            // Subtitle Text (Bottom Center)
            subtitleText = CreateText("SubtitleText", canvas.transform, defaultFont, 22, TextAnchor.LowerCenter);
            RectTransform subRect = subtitleText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            subRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0.5f, 0f);
            subRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0f);
            subRect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0f);
            subRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 48f);
            subRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(1300f, 140f);
            subtitleText.text = string.Empty;

            // Document Modal Panel
            documentPanel = CreateModalPanel("DocumentModalPanel", canvas.transform, new Vector2(880f, 520f));
            documentTitleText = CreateText("DocumentTitle", documentPanel.transform, defaultFont, 26, TextAnchor.UpperCenter);
            RectTransform docTitleRect = documentTitleText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            docTitleRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 200f);
            docTitleRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(800f, 50f);
            documentTitleText.color = new Color(1f, 0.85f, 0.4f);

            documentBodyText = CreateText("DocumentBody", documentPanel.transform, defaultFont, 21, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
            RectTransform docBodyRect = documentBodyText.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            docBodyRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 15f);
            docBodyRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(780f, 300f);

            documentCloseButton = CreateButton("CloseDocumentButton", documentPanel.transform, defaultFont, "[TUTUP DOKUMEN]", new Vector2(0f, -195f), new Vector2(260f, 50f), CloseDocumentModal);
            documentPanel.SetActive(false);

            // Padlock 3-Digit Modal Panel (Code 1-4-8)
            padlockPanel = CreateModalPanel("PadlockModalPanel", canvas.transform, new Vector2(680f, 460f));
            Text padlockHeader = CreateText("PadlockHeader", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, 25, TextAnchor.UpperCenter);
            padlockHeader.GetComponent<RectTransform>().anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 175f);
            padlockHeader.GetComponent<RectTransform>().sizeDelta = new Vector2(600f, 50f);
            padlockHeader.text = "GEMBOK ANGKA 3 DIGIT — LACI MEJA KERJA";

            padlockDigitsText = CreateText("PadlockDigits", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, 44, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            padlockDigitsText.GetComponent<RectTransform>().anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, 40f);
            padlockDigitsText.GetComponent<RectTransform>().sizeDelta = new Vector2(460f, 80f);
            padlockDigitsText.color = new Color(0.95f, 0.9f, 0.55f);

            padlockFeedbackText = CreateText("PadlockFeedback", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, 19, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            padlockFeedbackText.GetComponent<RectTransform>().anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, -55f);
            padlockFeedbackText.GetComponent<RectTransform>().sizeDelta = new Vector2(580f, 45f);

            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                int index = i;
                float xPos = (i - 1) * 140f;
                CreateButton($"DigitUp_{i}", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, "+", new Vector2(xPos, 110f), new Vector2(80f, 42f), () => AdjustDigit(index, 1));
                CreateButton($"DigitDown_{i}", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, "-", new Vector2(xPos, -20f), new Vector2(80f, 42f), () => AdjustDigit(index, -1));
            }

            CreateButton("UnlockPadlockBtn", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, "[BUKA GEMBOK]", new Vector2(-120f, -155f), new Vector2(210f, 48f), SubmitPadlockCode);
            CreateButton("CancelPadlockBtn", padlockPanel.transform, defaultFont, "[BATAL]", new Vector2(120f, -155f), new Vector2(210f, 48f), ClosePadlockModal);
            padlockPanel.SetActive(false);
        }

        public void SetObjective(string objective)
        {
            if (objectiveText != null)
            {
                objectiveText.text = string.IsNullOrEmpty(objective) ? string.Empty : $"OBJEKTIF: {objective}";
            }
        }

        public void SetInteractionPrompt(string prompt)
        {
            if (interactionPromptText != null)
            {
                interactionPromptText.text = prompt;
            }
        }

        public void EnqueueSubtitle(string speaker, string text, float duration = 4.5f)
        {
            _subtitleQueue.Enqueue((speaker, text, duration));
            if (_subtitleRoutine == null)
            {
                _subtitleRoutine = StartCoroutine(ProcessSubtitlesRoutine());
            }
        }

        public void ShowBroadcastNotification(string message, float duration = 8f)
        {
            if (broadcastToastText == null) return;
            StopCoroutine(nameof(ClearBroadcastRoutine));
            broadcastToastText.text = message;
            StartCoroutine(ClearBroadcastRoutine(duration));
        }

        public void UpdatePhoneStatusUI(SmartphoneController phone)
        {
            if (phoneStatusText == null || phone == null)
                return;

            if (!phone.hasSmartphoneInInventory)
            {
                phoneStatusText.text = "[HP BELUM DIAMBIL]";
                return;
            }

            string screenState = phone.isScreenOn ? "LAYAR NYALA" : "LAYAR MATI [HEMAT DAYA]";
            string holderState = phone.isPhoneRaised ? "DIANGKAT [TAB]" : "DISIMPAN [TAB]";
            phoneStatusText.text =
                $"SMARTPHONE RAKA ({holderState})\n" +
                $"Baterai: {phone.batteryPercent:0}% | {screenState} [F]\n" +
                $"Mode Visual: {phone.currentScanMode}";
        }

        public void OpenDocumentModal(string title, string body, Action onClosed = null)
        {
            if (documentPanel == null) return;
            _onDocumentClosed = onClosed;
            documentTitleText.text = title;
            documentBodyText.text = body;
            documentPanel.SetActive(true);
            SetPlayerLockForModal(true);
        }

        public void CloseDocumentModal()
        {
            if (documentPanel == null) return;
            documentPanel.SetActive(false);
            SetPlayerLockForModal(false);
            Action callback = _onDocumentClosed;
            _onDocumentClosed = null;
            callback?.Invoke();
        }

        public void OpenPadlockModal(string targetCode, Action onSolved)
        {
            if (padlockPanel == null) return;
            _targetPadlockCode = targetCode;
            _onPadlockSolved = onSolved;
            _digits[0] = 0;
            _digits[1] = 0;
            _digits[2] = 0;
            padlockFeedbackText.text = "Masukkan 3 digit kombinasi gembok laci.";
            RefreshPadlockDisplay();
            padlockPanel.SetActive(true);
            SetPlayerLockForModal(true);
        }

        public void ClosePadlockModal()
        {
            if (padlockPanel == null) return;
            padlockPanel.SetActive(false);
            SetPlayerLockForModal(false);
        }

        public void SetFadeAlpha(float alpha)
        {
            if (blackFadeImage != null)
            {
                Color c = blackFadeImage.color;
                c.a = Mathf.Clamp01(alpha);
                blackFadeImage.color = c;
            }
        }

        public Coroutine FadeBlack(float fromAlpha, float toAlpha, float duration)
        {
            return StartCoroutine(FadeRoutine(fromAlpha, toAlpha, duration));
        }

        private void AdjustDigit(int slot, int delta)
        {
            _digits[slot] = (_digits[slot] + delta + 10) % 10;
            RefreshPadlockDisplay();
        }

        private void RefreshPadlockDisplay()
        {
            if (padlockDigitsText != null)
            {
                padlockDigitsText.text = $"[ {_digits[0]} ]   -   [ {_digits[1]} ]   -   [ {_digits[2]} ]";
            }
        }

        private void SubmitPadlockCode()
        {
            string entered = $"{_digits[0]}{_digits[1]}{_digits[2]}";
            if (entered == _targetPadlockCode)
            {
                ClosePadlockModal();
                _onPadlockSolved?.Invoke();
            }
            else if (padlockFeedbackText != null)
            {
                padlockFeedbackText.text = $"Kode {entered} salah. Gembok masih terkunci.";
            }
        }

        private void SetPlayerLockForModal(bool modalOpen)
        {
            FirstPersonController fpp = FindFirstObjectByType<FirstPersonController>();
            if (fpp != null)
            {
                fpp.SetCursorLock(!modalOpen);
            }
        }

        private IEnumerator ProcessSubtitlesRoutine()
        {
            while (_subtitleQueue.Count > 0)
            {
                var (speaker, line, duration) = _subtitleQueue.Dequeue();
                if (subtitleText != null)
                {
                    subtitleText.text = string.IsNullOrEmpty(speaker)
                        ? line
                        : $"{speaker}: \"{line}\"";
                }
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(duration);
            }

            if (subtitleText != null)
                subtitleText.text = string.Empty;

            _subtitleRoutine = null;
        }

        private IEnumerator ClearBroadcastRoutine(float delay)
        {
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(delay);
            if (broadcastToastText != null)
                broadcastToastText.text = string.Empty;
        }

        private IEnumerator FadeRoutine(float fromAlpha, float toAlpha, float duration)
        {
            SetFadeAlpha(fromAlpha);
            float elapsed = 0f;
            while (elapsed < duration)
            {
                elapsed += Time.deltaTime;
                SetFadeAlpha(Mathf.Lerp(fromAlpha, toAlpha, elapsed / Mathf.Max(0.01f, duration)));
                yield return null;
            }
            SetFadeAlpha(toAlpha);
        }

        private static Font LoadSafeFont()
        {
            Font font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
            if (font == null) font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("Arial.ttf");
            if (font == null) font = Font.CreateDynamicFontFromOSFont("Liberation Sans", 18);
            return font;
        }

        private static GameObject CreateUIObject(string name, Transform parent)
        {
            GameObject go = new GameObject(name);
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            go.AddComponent<RectTransform>();
            return go;
        }

        private static void StretchFull(RectTransform rect)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rect.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        }

        private static Text CreateText(string name, Transform parent, Font font, int size, TextAnchor anchor)
        {
            GameObject go = CreateUIObject(name, parent);
            Text txt = go.AddComponent<Text>();
            txt.font = font;
            txt.fontSize = size;
            txt.alignment = anchor;
            txt.color = Color.white;
            txt.horizontalOverflow = HorizontalWrapMode.Wrap;
            txt.verticalOverflow = VerticalWrapMode.Overflow;
            txt.raycastTarget = false;
            return txt;
        }

        private static GameObject CreateModalPanel(string name, Transform parent, Vector2 size)
        {
            GameObject panel = CreateUIObject(name, parent);
            RectTransform rect = panel.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            rect.sizeDelta = size;
            Image bg = panel.AddComponent<Image>();
            bg.color = new Color(0.06f, 0.06f, 0.08f, 0.95f);
            return panel;
        }

        private static Button CreateButton(string name, Transform parent, Font font, string label, Vector2 pos, Vector2 size, UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction onClick)
        {
            GameObject btnObj = CreateUIObject(name, parent);
            RectTransform rect = btnObj.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            rect.anchoredPosition = pos;
            rect.sizeDelta = size;

            Image img = btnObj.AddComponent<Image>();
            img.color = new Color(0.22f, 0.22f, 0.26f, 1f);

            Button btn = btnObj.AddComponent<Button>();
            btn.targetGraphic = img;
            btn.onClick.AddListener(onClick);

            Text txt = CreateText("Label", btnObj.transform, font, 18, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter);
            StretchFull(txt.GetComponent<RectTransform>());
            txt.text = label;
            return btn;
        }
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Prolog/KosInteractableItem.cs`

Komponen objek interaktif yang meneruskan interaksi ke `KamarKosPrologDirector` berdasarkan ID objek di Kamar Kos Raka.

```csharp
using LayarTerakhir.Player;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Prolog
{
    public class KosInteractableItem : MonoBehaviour, IInteractable
    {
        public string itemId;
        public string promptLabel;
        public bool isInteractable = true;
        public KamarKosPrologDirector director;

        public string InteractableId => itemId;

        public string GetInteractionPrompt()
        {
            return promptLabel;
        }

        public bool CanInteract()
        {
            return isInteractable && gameObject.activeInHierarchy;
        }

        public void Interact()
        {
            if (director != null)
            {
                director.OnObjectInteracted(this);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

### `Assets/_LayarTerakhir/Scripts/Prolog/KamarKosPrologDirector.cs`

Mengatur seluruh alur naratif dan teka-teki **Prolog Adegan 1: Kamar Kos Raka (Menit 00:00 – 12:00)** sesuai dokumen `Prolog.MD`:

1. Layar hitam perlahan membuka di depan meja PC ("Malam Keramat" — 32 penonton), monolog pembuka Raka, ketukan keras 3 kali di pintu kos, dan objektif `Periksa bawah pintu kamar.`

2. Membaca **Surat Peringatan Terakhir Bu Haji Ratna** (`Rp3.500.000`), monolog Raka, LED merah HP berkedip di atas kasur, dan objektif `Ambil Smartphone di atas kasur.`

3. Mengambil Smartphone: menampilkan 14 Panggilan Tak Terjawab dari "Damar (Kameramen)" (21:15 WIB), memutar *Voice Note* Damar (22 detik), *glitch* layar 1 detik, notifikasi donasi masuk `Rp5.000.000` dari `Penonton_Terakhir`, dan objektif `Ambil Kunci Motor, Jaket, dan Baterai Cadangan sebelum berangkat.`

4. Menyelesaikan puzzle gembok laci `1 - 4 - 8` berdasarkan *Sticky Note* di meja PC dan *Kalender Dinding* (14 Agustus — Ultah Ibu), mengambil **Kunci Motor**, **1x Baterai Cadangan (Powerbank Kecil)**, dan **Jaket** di balik pintu, lalu keluar kamar kos dengan *Fade to Black* dan penyimpanan checkpoint atomik.



```csharp
using System.Collections;
using LayarTerakhir.Core;
using LayarTerakhir.Smartphone;
using LayarTerakhir.UI;
using UnityEngine;

namespace LayarTerakhir.Prolog
{
    public enum KosPrologStage
    {
        WakeUpAndKnocks = 0,
        WaitReadSuratIbuKos = 1,
        WaitPickupSmartphone = 2,
        PreparationAndDrawerPuzzle = 3,
        ReadyToDepart = 4,
        Completed = 5
    }

    public class KamarKosPrologDirector : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const string DrawerPasscode = "148";

        [Header("Progression State")]
        public KosPrologStage currentStage = KosPrologStage.WakeUpAndKnocks;
        public bool hasJacket = false;
        public bool hasMotorKey = false;
        public bool hasSpareBattery = false;
        public bool isDrawerUnlocked = false;

        [Header("Scene Object References")]
        public KosInteractableItem pcMonitorItem;
        public KosInteractableItem suratKosItem;
        public KosInteractableItem smartphoneBedItem;
        public KosInteractableItem stickyNoteItem;
        public KosInteractableItem wallCalendarItem;
        public KosInteractableItem lockedDrawerItem;
        public KosInteractableItem jacketItem;
        public KosInteractableItem kosDoorItem;
        public Light phoneBedRedLed;

        private void Start()
        {
            if (CampaignState.Instance != null)
            {
                CampaignState.Instance.StartNewGame();
            }

            // Surat kos & HP belum diaktifkan sebelum urutannya
            if (suratKosItem != null) suratKosItem.isInteractable = false;
            if (smartphoneBedItem != null) smartphoneBedItem.isInteractable = false;
            if (phoneBedRedLed != null) phoneBedRedLed.enabled = false;

            StartCoroutine(BeginOpeningSequenceRoutine());
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (phoneBedRedLed != null && phoneBedRedLed.enabled)
            {
                phoneBedRedLed.intensity = 1.5f + Mathf.Sin(Time.time * 12f) * 1.5f;
            }
        }

        private IEnumerator BeginOpeningSequenceRoutine()
        {
            if (HudController.Instance == null)
                yield break;

            // Kondisi Awal: Layar hitam pekat, suara hujan deras & dengung kipas PC tua, mata Raka terbuka perlahan
            HudController.Instance.SetFadeAlpha(1f);
            HudController.Instance.SetObjective(string.Empty);
            HudController.Instance.EnqueueSubtitle(
                "SFX",
                "[Suara hujan deras menghantam atap seng & dengung kipas PC tua]",
                2.5f
            );

            yield return HudController.Instance.FadeBlack(1f, 0f, 3.0f);

            // 1. Monolog Pembuka & Interaksi Meja PC
            HudController.Instance.EnqueueSubtitle(
                "Raka",
                "Tiga puluh dua penonton... Itu pun paling setengahnya akun bot. Kalau malam ini nggak ada konten yang meledak, tamat riwayat gue di Jakarta.",
                5.0f
            );
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(5.2f);

            HudController.Instance.EnqueueSubtitle(
                "SFX",
                "[TOK! TOK! TOK! Ketukan keras 3 kali di pintu kayu kamar kos, disusul langkah kaki berat menjauh]",
                3.5f
            );
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(3.6f);

            currentStage = KosPrologStage.WaitReadSuratIbuKos;
            if (suratKosItem != null)
                suratKosItem.isInteractable = true;

            HudController.Instance.SetObjective("Periksa bawah pintu kamar.");
        }

        public void OnObjectInteracted(KosInteractableItem item)
        {
            if (item == null || HudController.Instance == null)
                return;

            switch (item.itemId)
            {
                case "PC_MONITOR":
                    HandleInspectPCMonitor();
                    break;
                case "SURAT_KOS":
                    HandleReadSuratIbuKos(item);
                    break;
                case "SMARTPHONE_BED":
                    HandlePickupSmartphone(item);
                    break;
                case "STICKY_NOTE":
                    HandleInspectStickyNote();
                    break;
                case "KALENDER_DINDING":
                    HandleInspectCalendar();
                    break;
                case "LACI_MEJA":
